公司簡介
Corporate Profile

                                                      綠城中國控股有限公司是中國領先的優質房產品開發及生活
                                                      綜合服務供應商，以優秀的產品質量、獨特的建築美學、
                                                      以人為本的服務保持行業引領地位。公司連續二十二年榮
                                                      獲「中國房地產百強企業綜合實力 Top10」1，所進駐重點城
                                                      市客戶滿意度連續十六年保持領先，並連續五年榮獲產品力
                                                      測評榜第一，此外還獲得「中國房地產年度社會責任感企業
                                                      Top2」1、「中國房企品牌價值 Top4」2 等榮譽。

                                                      Greentown China Holdings Limited is a leading quality
                                                      property developer and an integrated living service provider
                                                      in China. It maintains a leadership position in the industry by
                                                      virtue of the quality of its properties, its unique architectural
                                                      aesthetics, and its customer-centric services. The Company
                                                      has been awarded the “Top10 among 100 Chinese Real
                                                      Estate Enterprises by Comprehensive Strength” 1 for 22
                                                      consecutive years and maintained a leading position in
                                                      customer satisfaction in core cities in which it operates for
                                                      16 consecutive years as well as ranked first in the product
                                                      strength assessment lists for five consecutive years. It has also
                                                      received honors such as “Top2 Chinese Real Estate Enterprises
                                                      by Social Responsibility” 1 and “Top4 Chinese Real Estate
                                                      Companies by Brand Value”2.

                                                      綠城中國控股有限公司於 1995 年 1 月在中國杭州成立，2006 年 7 月在香港聯交所
                                                      上市，並先後引入九龍倉、中交集團作為重要股東。於本報告日期，綠城中國的
                                                      最大單一股東中交集團持有本公司已發行股本總額約 28.88% 的權益。

                                                      Greentown China Holdings Limited was founded in Hangzhou, China in January
                                                      1995 and listed on the Hong Kong Stock Exchange in July 2006. Wharf and CCCG
                                                      were introduced as substantial shareholders successively. As at the date of this
                                                      report, CCCG, Greentown China’s single largest shareholder, was interested in
                                                      approximately 28.88% of the total issued share capital of the Company.

                                                      附註:                                Notes:

                                                      1.       中指院                       1.       China Index Academy
慈溪鳳起潮鳴
                                                      2.       億翰智庫                      2.       EH Consulting
Cixi Fengqi Chaoming

歷經三十一年的發展，公司秉持「品質為先」的發      Throughout 31 years of development, the Company has adhered to the
展戰略指引，佈局房地產上下游業務，形成重資       development strategy of “quality first” and developed the upstream and
產、輕資產和「綠城＋」三大板塊。重資產板塊聚      downstream of the real estate business to form three major segments, namely
焦房產開發業務，持續精進產品力，打造綠城「好      asset-heavy, asset-light and “Greentown+”. The asset-heavy segment focuses
  ；輕資產板塊強化代建業務，2020 年 7 月
房子」                         on the real estate development business, and continuously enhances product

上市（股票代碼：09979），市佔率在行業中遙遙    the project management business. In July 2020, Greentown Management
領先；「綠城＋」板塊發展和培育不動產產業鏈業      Holdings Company Limited was officially spun off and listed separately on the
務，建立產業生態圈，協同賦能主業。           Main Board of the Stock Exchange (stock code: 09979), with a market share
                            ranking far ahead in the industry. The “Greentown+” segment develops and
                            cultivates the industry chain business of real estate and establishes an industrial
                            ecosystem to collaborate and empower its main business.

經營回顧                                   Operation Review
2026 年上半年，房地產市場延續調整態勢。中央               In the first half of 2026, the real estate market remained in an ongoing
和地方圍繞「努力穩定房地產市場」的明確導向精                 adjustment phase. The central and local governments implemented precise
準施策，同時需求端持續釋放，市場呈現結構性                  measures under the clear guidance of “striving to stabilize the real estate
修復的特徵，部分核心城市新房及二手房的成交                  market”, while demand continued to be released. The market showed signs
量價均有所回升，但分化格局進一步加深，市場                  of a structural recovery; transaction volumes and prices of new and second-
整體仍處於低位盤整。從統計數據來看，行業規                  hand homes in certain core cities rebounded, yet divergence intensified and
模仍在收縮，全國新建商品房銷售金額和銷售面                  the overall market remained in a low-level consolidation phase. According to
積同比降幅進一步擴大；投資仍不樂觀，開發投                  statistical data, the industry is still undergoing scale contraction, with year-
資降幅繼續下探，房企開發到位資金明顯下滑；                  on-year declines in sales amount and sales area of national new commercial
土地供應縮量分化，核心區位土拍競爭加劇。因                  housing widening further; investment sentiment remained subdued as decline
此，整體且可持續的市場回穩仍面臨較大的壓力。                 in real estate development investment continued to deepen, and property
                                       developers’ received development funding fell significantly; land supply
                                       contracted and diverged across regions, while competition in land auctions of
                                       core areas intensified. Accordingly, a comprehensive and sustainable market
                                       stabilization still faces considerable pressure.

流安全為核心，持續固本強基，提質增效。上半                  2030 plan, firmly placed cash flow security as the cornerstone, and continued
年， 公 司 各 項 經 營 舉 措 平 穩、 有 序、 高 效 的 落   to strengthen foundations and enhance quality and efficiency. In the first half
實，銷售排名穩居前列，新增投資守住質量，經                  of the year, the Company’s various operating initiatives were carried out in a
營發展以穩促進；同時，財務狀況安全穩健，品                  steady, orderly and efficient manner. The sales rankings remained in the top
質優勢持續穩固，公司發展底盤進一步夯實。綠                  ranks, newly-added investments maintained quality standards, and operating
城中國連續 22 年榮膺「中國房地產百強企業綜合               development was advanced through stability. Meanwhile, the financial
實力 Top10」，並憑借穩健經營、產品力及交付力              position remained safe and sound, quality advantages remained solid, and the

底修復，對房企而言，機遇與挑戰並存。政策端                expected to continue bottoming out and recovering. For real estate developers,
將圍繞「穩定房地產市場」持續發力，市場端行                opportunities and challenges coexist. On the policy front, efforts will keep
業整體規模體量依然龐大，供求關係將進一步改                focusing on “stabilizing the real estate market”. On the market front, the overall
善。同時，不同區域及城市、產品需求以及市場                scale of the industry is still substantial and the supply-demand relationship will
結構的分化預計將進一步加劇。核心城市憑借優                further improve. At the same time, differentiation across regions and cities,
質供給、政策支持以及較強的產業發展動能等因                product demand and market structure is expected to intensify further. Core
素，企穩基礎或將進一步增強，但市場全面回暖                cities, leveraging quality supply, policy support and relatively strong industrial
仍面臨壓力。                               development momentum, may see their stabilization foundations further
                                     strengthened, yet a full market recovery still faces pressure.

1.   公司資料                                           1.   CORPORATE INFORMATION
     綠城中國控股有限公司（「本公司」）於 2005                             Greentown China Holdings Limited (the “Company”) was incorporated in
     年 8 月 31 日根據公司法（2004 年修訂本）於                         the Cayman Islands on 31 August 2005 as an exempted company with
     開曼群島註冊成立為一家獲豁免有限公司，                                 limited liability under the Companies Law (2004 Revision) and its shares
     其股份於 2006 年 7 月 13 日於香港聯合交易                         have been listed on the Main Board of The Stock Exchange of Hong Kong
     所有限公司（「聯交所」）主板上市。                                   Limited (the “Stock Exchange”) with effect from 13 July 2006.


     本公司乃投資控股公司。其附屬公司（連同                                 The Company is an investment holding company. The principal activity of
     本公司統稱為「本集團」）的主要業務為於中                                its subsidiaries (together with the Company referred to as the “Group”) is
     國發展供銷售住宅物業。                                         the development for sale of residential properties in the PRC.


2.   編製基準                                           2.   BASIS OF PREPARATION
     本中期財務報告乃根據香港聯合交易所有限                                 This interim financial report has been prepared in accordance with
     公司證券上市規則的適用披露條文編製，包                                 the applicable disclosure provisions of the Rules Governing the Listing
     括遵守國際會計準則理事會頒佈的國際會計                                 of Securities on The Stock Exchange of Hong Kong Limited, including
       「國際會計準則」）第 34 號：中期財務
     準則（                                                 compliance with International Accounting Standards (“IAS”) 34, Interim
     報告。其於 2026 年 8 月 25 日獲授權刊發。                         financial reporting, issued by the International Accounting Standards Board
                                                         (IASB). It was authorised for issue on 25 August 2026.


     除預計將於 2026 年年度財務報表中反映的                              The interim financial report has been prepared in accordance with the
     會計政策變動外，中期財務報告已根據與                                  same accounting policies adopted in the 2025 annual financial statements,
     2025 年年度財務報表所採納的相同會計政策                              except for the accounting policy changes that are expected to be reflected
     編製。任何會計政策變動詳情載於附註 3。                                in the 2026 annual financial statements. Details of any changes in
                                                         accounting policies are set out in note 3.


     中期財務報告已符合國際會計準則第 34 號                               The preparation of an interim financial report in conformity with IAS 34
     編製，要求管理層作出影響政策應用及所呈                                 requires management to make judgements, estimates, and assumptions
     報截至結算日之資產與負債、收入與開支金                                 that affect the application of policies and reported amounts of assets and
     額之判斷、估計及假設。實際結果可能有別                                 liabilities, income and expenses on a year-to-date basis. Actual results may
     於該等估計。                                              differ from these estimates.


     中期財務報告載有中期財務報表及所選說明                                 This interim financial report contains interim financial statements and
     附註。該等附註包括就理解本集團自 2025                               selected explanatory notes. The notes include an explanation of events
     年年度財務報表以來的財務狀況及表現變動                                 and transactions that are significant to an understanding of the changes
     而言屬重大的事件及交易的解釋。簡明綜合                                 in financial position and performance of the Group since the 2025 annual

     所規定的全部資料。                                           required for a full set of financial statements prepared in accordance with

3.   會計政策變動                                         3.   CHANGES IN ACCOUNTING POLICIES
     國際會計準則理事會已就本會計期間首次生                                 The IASB has issued a number of amendments to IFRS Accounting
     效的國際財務報告準則會計準則頒佈多項修                                 Standards that are first effective for the current accounting period. Of
     訂本。其中僅國際財務報告準則第 9 號修訂                               these, only the amendments to IFRS 9, Financial instruments and IFRS 7,
     本「金融工具」及國際財務報告準則第 7 號                               Financial instruments: Disclosures – Amendments to the classification and
     修訂本「金融工具：披露 – 金融工具分類及                               measurement of financial instruments , are relevant to the group’s financial

     •     該等修訂本澄清金融資產或金融負債                              •     The amendments clarify when a financial asset or a financial liability
           何時確認及終止確認。其亦引入一項                                    is recognised and derecognised. They also introduce an optional
           選擇性例外情況，允許實體在金融負                                    exception that permits an entity to derecognise a financial liability

           況下，於結算日期之前終止確認金融                                    cash using an electronic payment system, provided that specific

     •     就評估金融資產是否有合約現金流量                              •    For the assessment of whether a financial asset has contractual
         （僅為支付本金及未償還本金利息）而                                    cash flows that are solely payments of principal and interest on

           用評估的合約關連工具之間的區別。                                   features and contractually linked instruments which may then

     於採納該等修訂本後，本集團已選擇應用豁                                 Upon adoption of the amendments, the Group has elected to apply the
     免終止確認使用合資格電子支付系統以現金                                 exception for the derecognition of certain trade payables settled in cash
     結算的若干貿易應付款項。在例外情況下，                                 using qualifying electronic payment systems. Under the exception, these
     當本集團通過合資格電子支付系統啓動支付                                 trade payables are derecognised when the Group initiates a payment
     指示時，該等貿易應付款項將終止確認，因                                 instruction through a qualifying electronic payment system and, as a

     獲取用於結算的現金的實際能力，且與支付                                 payment instruction and to access the cash to be used for settlement, and
     系統相關的結算風險並不重大。本集團已一                                 the settlement risk associated with the payment system is insignificant.
     致將此選擇應用於通過相同合資格電子支付                                 The Group has applied this election consistently to all settlements made
     系統作出的所有結算。                                          through the same qualifying electronic payment system.

           地預測本集團有權收取的代價金額。                                                most likely amount, depending on which method better predicts the
                                                                           amount of consideration to which the Group will be entitled.

     民企業的合資格股息收入繳納所得稅。除此                               enterprises that have a direct investment relationship is exempted from
     之外，該等股息須按企業所得稅法繳付 5%                              income tax. Otherwise, such dividends will be subject to a 5% or 10%
     或 10% 預扣稅。5% 或 10% 的預扣稅率適用                        withholding tax under the EIT Law. A 5% or 10% withholding tax rate is
     於本集團。期內，根據本集團的營運及發展                               applicable to the Group. During the period, the directors of the Company,
     計劃，本公司董事估計中國附屬公司保留盈                               based on the Group’s operation and expansion plan, estimated that part
     利的部分將留存在中國內地，以作日後營運                               of the retained earnings of subsidiaries in the PRC would be retained in
     及投資之用。董事認為，該等附屬公司於可                               Chinese Mainland for use in future operations and investments. In the
     見將來分派的盈利，將少於報告期末已確認                               opinion of the directors, the earnings which will be distributed by these

30. 金融工具的公平值及公平值等級                                  30. FAIR VALUE AND FAIR VALUE HIERARCHY OF FINANCIAL
                                                        INSTRUMENTS
     管理層已評估，現金及現金等價物、受限制                               Management has assessed that the fair values of cash and cash
     銀行存款、貿易及其他應收款項、訂金及預                               equivalents, restricted bank deposits, trade and other receivables, deposits
     付款項、應收╱應付關聯人士款項、貿易及                               and prepayments, amounts due from/to related parties, trade and other
     其他應付款項以及銀行及其他借款流動部分                               payables, the current portion of bank and other borrowings approximate
     的公平值與其賬面值相若，主要由於該等工                               to their carrying amounts largely due to the short-term maturities of these
     具於短期內到期。                                          instruments.


     管理層負責釐定金融工具公平值計量的政策                               Management is responsible for determining the policies and procedures
     及程序。於各報告日期，管理層分析金融工                               for the fair value measurement of financial instruments. At each reporting
     具價值的變動情況，並釐定估值中所用的主                               date, management analyses the movements in the values of financial
     要輸入數據。估值過程及結果經董事會一年                               instruments and determines the major inputs applied in the valuation. The
     討論兩次，以進行中期及年度財務匯報。                                valuation process and results are discussed with the board of directors
                                                       twice a year for interim and annual financial reporting.


     金融資產及負債的公平值計入自願雙方可於                               The fair values of the financial assets and liabilities are included at
     當前交易（脅迫或清盤銷售除外）中交換該工                              the amount at which the instrument could be exchanged in a current
     具所需的金額。估計公平值使用以下方法及                               transaction between willing parties, other than in a forced or liquidation
     假設：                                               sale. The following methods and assumptions were used to estimate the
                                                       fair values:


     上市權益投資的公平值基於已報市場價格。                               The fair values of listed equity investments are based on quoted market
     董事認為，估值技術產生的估計公平值（計                               prices. The directors believe that the estimated fair values resulting from
     入中期簡明綜合財務狀況表）以及相關公平                               the valuation technique, which are recorded in the interim condensed
     值變動（計入其他全面收益）均屬合理，且為                              consolidated statement of financial position, and the related changes
     報告期末的最恰當值。                                        in fair values, which are recorded in other comprehensive income, are
                                                       reasonable, and that they were the most appropriate values at the end of
                                                       the reporting period.


     就按公平值計入其他全面收益的未上市權益                               For the fair value of the unlisted equity investments at fair value through
     投資的公平值而言，管理層已估計使用合理                               other comprehensive income, management has estimated the potential
     變數作為評估模式的輸入數據的潛在影響。                               effect of using reasonably possible alternatives as inputs to the valuation
                                                       model.

      有效對沖工具的貨幣掉期合同，以管理本集                              currency swap contract designated as highly effective hedging instrument

     列與披露」
     國際財務報告準則第 18 號將取代國際會計                             IFRS 18 will replace IAS 1, Presentation of financial statements , and is first
     準則第 1 號「財務報表的呈列」，並於 2027                          effective for annual reporting periods beginning on or after 1 January 2027.
     年 1 月 1 日或之後開始的年度報告期間首次
     生效。

     國際財務報告準則第 18 號引入於財務報表                             IFRS 18 introduces new requirements for presentation and disclosure in
     內呈列及披露的新規定，包括於損益表加入                               financial statements, including new categories and defined subtotals in
     新類別及界定小計、加強匯總及分類資料的                               the statement of profit or loss, enhanced requirements for aggregation
     要求以及有關管理層界定績效計量的具體披                               and disaggregation of information, and specific disclosures about
     露。本集團正在評估首次應用國際財務報告                               management-defined performance measures. The group is assessing
     準則第 18 號將對其綜合財務報表產生的預                             the expected impact that the initial application of IFRS 18 will have on its
     期影響。                                              consolidated financial statements.

     本集團預期採納國際財務報告準則第 18 號                             The Group expects that the adoption of IFRS 18 will not change the group’s
     不會改變本集團的資產淨值或純利。然而，                               net assets or net profit. However, it is expected to affect the presentation
     其預期將影響本集團綜合損益表及相關附註                               of the Group’s consolidated statement of profit or loss and related note
     披露的呈列。由於本集團仍在持續進行實施                               disclosures. The assessment is still undergoing as the Group continues its
     工作，評估目前仍在進行中。                                     implementation work.
